Two actual DMA engines, real CQ/MMIO, software behavior supplied by C++; no CPU/Linux execution. Deterministic FIFO/head-only behavioral DDR. AR-to-first-R=17 requires an empty queue and ready receiver. A includes final flush, B ends at hardware COPYDONE; makespan=max, distinct from sum; CPU PTE-update instructions excluded. Test-only PR/PW overlay; fixed accepted completion-interface cohort and model DDR RLAST/B are not a universal physical ordering model or full safety proof. Separate partitioned P/PN DS-first/F4/startup31 fixture; T_return includes cold start and quiet, unlike main device times. |

## Platform diagram facts (F001)

The parent platform's CVA6/four-DMA context is sourced from `iommu-platform/README.md:3-5`; the exercised two DMA instances are `runner/invalidation_dma_top.sv:132-140`. The visible diagram labels A64/B256 come from every old CSV row's `A_jobs=64,B_jobs=256`; `copy_bytes=4096` is the associated source workload, not a visible diagram label. CPU/Linux are contextual, not executed. CQ/translation/memory connections are the exercised harness sources listed in F001.

## Index checks and limits

Every one of 145 current claim IDs has an explicit nonempty source binding; all source paths/ranges/selectors and columns resolve. All 52 macro values were recomputed from source CSV cells; all original fields in 28 invalidation, 18 PLRU and 28 mainline/diagnosis derived rows match their recorded source lines/hashes. All six table rows match the CSV; every cited bibliography key has an evidence binding. The claim index retains inference/recommendation limits and does not certify complete evidence sufficiency or publication readiness.
